       industrial deployment. AI is no longer evaluated on basic                已由基礎文本生成與通用問答能力，快速轉

       support tool into next-generation infrastructure that is                 日，商務部等九部門發佈《關於加快零售業創
       reshaping how industries organize, operate, and deliver.                 新發展的意見》，以擴大內需為基點，引導零
       On July 9, 2026, the Ministry of Commerce, together with                 售業態融合轉型、加快數智化升級與供應鏈

The functional currency of the Company is United States        本公司的功能貨幣為美元（「美元」）。由於本
Dollars (“USD” or “US$”). The consolidated financial           集團大部分業務由本公司於中國的附屬公司
statements are presented in RMB as the majority of the         開展而其功能貨幣為人民幣，故綜合財務報
Group’s operations are conducted by the Company’s              表以人民幣呈列。本集團經營業務的其他貨
subsidiaries established in the PRC and the functional         幣之間的匯率波動可能影響本集團的財務狀
currency of which is RMB. Fluctuations in exchange rates       況及經營業績，而本集團的外幣風險主要因
between other currencies in which the Group conducts           美元與人民幣之間的匯率波動而產生。於本
its business may affect the Group’s financial position and     報告日期，本集團並無為減低我們面對的外
operation results and the Group’s foreign currency risk is     幣匯兌風險而進行任何對沖交易。然而，本
mainly due to exchange rate fluctuations between USD           集團管理層將通過定期檢討管理外幣匯兌風
and RMB. As at the date of this report, the Group has not      險，並考慮在未來進行對沖交易。

     2    CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                   2   會計政策變動
          The IASB has issued a number of amendments to IFRS                   國際會計準則理事會已頒佈若干國際財務報
          Accounting Standards that are first effective for the current        告準則會計準則修訂本，已於本會計期間首
          accounting period. Of these, only the amendments to IFRS             次生效。其中，僅國際財務報告準則第9號
          9, Financial instruments and IFRS 7, Financial instruments:          的修訂本金融工具及國際財務報告準則第7
          Disclosures – Amendments to the classification and                   號的修訂本金融工具：披露－金融工具之分
          measurement of financial instruments, are relevant to the            類與計量之修訂與本集團之財務報表有關。
          Group’s financial statements. The impacts of adopting these          採納該等修訂的影響於下文討論。
          amendments are discussed below.


          The Group has not applied any new standard or interpretation         本集團並無應用任何於本會計期間尚未生效
          that is not yet effective for the current accounting period.         的新準則或詮釋。


          Amendments to IFRS 9, Financial instruments and IFRS 7,              國際財務報告準則第9號金融工具及國際財
          Financial instruments: Disclosures – Amendments to the               務 報 告 準 則 第 7 號 金 融 工 具： 披 露 之 修 訂
          classification and measurement of financial instruments.             本，金融工具之分類與計量之修訂。


          The amendments cover three main aspects:                             該等修訂涵蓋三個主要範疇：


          •     The amendments clarify when a financial asset or a             •   該等修訂闡明何時確認及終止確認金融
                financial liability is recognised and derecognized. They           資產或金融負債。同時亦引入一項可選
                also introduce an optional exception that permits an               擇的例外情況，倘符合特定標準，容許
                entity to derecognize a financial liability before the             實體在使用電子支付系統以現金結算金
                settlement date when the financial liability is settled            融負債時，於結算日期前終止確認有關
                in cash using an electronic payment system, provided               金融負債。
                that specific criteria are met.


          •     For the assessment of whether a financial asset has            •   就評估金融資產的合約現金流量是否僅
                contractual cash flows that are solely payments                    為支付本金及未償還本金金額之利息而
                of principal and interest on the principal amount                  言，該等修訂釐清了對利息的評估，並
                outstanding, the amendments clarify the assessment                 就具有或有特徵的金融資產（例如與環
                of interest and introduce an additional test for the               境、社會或管治（「ESG」）掛鈎的特徵）
                financial assets with contingent features, for example             引入一項額外測試。該等修訂亦闡明具
                environmental, social or governance (“ESG”)-linked                 有無追索權特徵的金融資產與合約掛鈎
                features. The amendments also clarify the difference               工具之間的差異，從而可能改變適用的
                between financial assets with non-recourse features                評估。
                and contractually linked instruments which may then
                change the applicable assessments.

         (d) Capital management                                            (d) 資本管理
             The Group’s primary objectives when managing                     本集團管理資本的主要目標是保障本集
             capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability to                  團持續經營的能力，從而通過與風險水
             continue as a going concern, so that it can continue             平相對應的產品及服務定價及以合理成
             to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for             本取得融資的方式，繼續為股東創造回
             other stakeholders, by pricing products and services             報及為其他持份者創造利益。
             commensurately with the level of risk and by securing
             access to finance at a reasonable cost.


             The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages             本集團積極定期審視及管理資本架構，
             its capital structure to maintain a balance between              以在較高股東回報情況下可能伴隨的較
             the higher shareholder returns that might be possible            高借貸水平與維持穩健資金狀況帶來的
             with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages              優勢及保障兩者間取得平衡，並根據經
             and security afforded by a sound capital position and            濟狀況的變動對資本架構作出調整。
             makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
             changes in economic conditions.


             The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis            本集團以資產負債率為基準監察其資本
             of debt-to-asset ratio. This ratio is calculated as total        架構。該比率按總負債除以總資產計
             liabilities divided by total assets.                             算。

         this interim financial report.


         The following updates the information provided in the last           上一年度財務報表所載國際財務報告準則第
         annual financial statements about the possible impacts of            18號於採納後或會對本集團綜合財務報表產
         IFRS 18 which may have a significant impact on the Group’s           生重大影響的資料進行下列更新。
         consolidated financial statements when adopted.


         IFRS 18, Presentation and disclosure in                              國際財務報告準則第18號，財務報
         financial statements                                                 表列報和披露
         IFRS 18 will replace IAS 1, Presentation of financial                國際財務報告準則第18號將取代國際會計準
         statements , and is first effective for annual reporting periods     則第1號財務報表的呈列，並於2027年1月1
         beginning on or after 1 January 2027.                                日或之後開始的年度報告期間首次生效。


         IFRS 18 introduces new requirements for presentation and             國際財務報告準則第18號就財務報表中的呈
         disclosure in financial statements, including new categories         列及披露引入新規定，包括於損益表中引入
         and defined subtotals in the statement of profit or loss,            新類別及界定小計、加強資料匯總及拆分的
         enhanced requirements for aggregation and disaggregation             規定，以及就管理層界定的績效指標作出特
         of information, and specific disclosures about management-           定披露。本集團現正評估首次應用國際財務
         defined performance measures. The Group is assessing the             報告準則第18號將對其綜合財務報表產生的
         expected impact that the initial application of IFRS 18 will         預期影響。
         have on its consolidated financial statements.


         The Group expects that the adoption of IFRS 18 will not              本集團預期採納國際財務報告準則第18號將
         change the Group’s net assets or net profit. However,                不會改變本集團的資產淨額或利潤淨額。然
         it is expected to affect the presentation of the Group’s             而，預期將影響本集團綜合損益表的列報及
         consolidated statement of profit or loss and related note            相關附註的披露。根據截至目前已進行的工
         disclosures. Based on the work performed to date, the                作，本集團對首次應用國際財務報告準則第
         Group’s assessment on the expected impact of the initial             18號的預期影響所作評估概述如下。該評估
         application of IFRS 18 is summarised below. The assessment           仍可能因本集團持續推進實施工作而有所變

   (b) Management-defined performance measures                      (b) 管理層界定的績效指標（「MPM」）
       (“MPM”)
         MPMs are subtotals of income and expenses used                   MPM指於財務報表以外的公開通訊中
         in public communications outside the financial                   使用的收入及開支小計，用以向使用者
         statements that communicate management’s view of                 傳達管理層對實體整體財務表現某一方
         an aspect of the financial performance of the entity             面的看法。本集團亦正在制定一套流

         a process to identify its public communications and              通訊中報告的其他計量是否符合MPM

         communications meet the definition of an MPM.

         financial statements. The MPMs disclosed by                      納國際財務報告準則第18號後披露的
         the Group following adoption of IFRS 18 will be                  MPM將根據本集團就相同報告期間刊
         determined based on public communications issued by              發的公開通訊釐定。
         the Group relating to the same reporting period.


   (c)   Principles of aggregation and disaggregation               (c)   匯總及分拆原則
         IFRS 18 provides enhanced principles on how to group             國際財務報告準則第18號就如何將財
         information in the financial statements, including               務報表（包括主要財務報表及附註）中的
         the primary financial statements and the notes. It               資料分組提供經加強的原則。其亦就於
         also introduces guidance on labelling and describing             主要財務報表中列報或於附註中披露的
         items presented in the primary financial statements or           項目的標示及描述引入指引。
         disclosed in the notes.
